Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Fix Deferred Condensation (ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE via TurnEnd)

## Context

PR #172 comment from khaong identified a critical bug: when a session in ACTIVE_COMMITTED phase receives `EventTurnEnd` (agent finishes its turn), the state machine emits `ActionCondense` but `transitionSessionTurnEnd()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:751` ignores the return value of `ApplyCommonActions()`. The condensation never fires. Session data from in-turn commits stays stuck on the shadow branch and never reaches `entire/sessions`.

This is the same class of silent-drop bug that ENT-250 (ActionHandler interface) would prevent at compile time.

## Bug Location

`hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:741-755`:
```go
func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
    // ...
    result := session.Transition(turnState.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
    session.ApplyCommonActions(turnState, result)  // ← return value IGNORED
    // ...
}
```

State machine transition (phase.go:227-231):
```
ACTIVE_COMMITTED + EventTurnEnd → PhaseIdle, [ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction]
```

`ActionCondense` is in `remaining` but never dispatched.

## Fix Approach: TurnEndHandler Optional Interface

Follow the established pattern for optional strategy interfaces (`PostCommitHandler`, `PrepareCommitMsgHandler`, `PrePushHandler`):

1. **New interface** `TurnEndHandler` in `strategy.go`:
   ```go
   type TurnEndHandler interface {
       HandleTurnEnd(state *session.State, actions []session.Action) error
   }
   ```
   The interface receives the state (already transitioned by `ApplyCommonActions`) and the `remaining` actions to dispatch. This keeps the interface general — the strategy decides what to do with each action.

2. **Implement on ManualCommitStrategy** in `manual_commit_hooks.go`:
   - For `ActionCondense`: open repo, derive checkpointID from `state.PendingCheckpointID`, check `hasNew`, call `condenseAndUpdateState` (reuse existing helper)
   - For other actions: log warning (unexpected at turn-end)
   - Shadow branch cleanup after condensation
   - State is saved by the caller (`transitionSessionTurnEnd`) after the handler returns

3. **Auto-commit strategy**: no-op (doesn't implement `TurnEndHandler`)

4. **Update `transitionSessionTurnEnd`** in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`:
   - Capture `remaining` from `ApplyCommonActions`
   - If `remaining` is non-empty, get strategy via `GetStrategy()`
   - Type-assert to `TurnEndHandler`, call `HandleTurnEnd`
   - Save state after handler returns

## Step-by-Step

### Step 1: Write failing test for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE condensation

New test in `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` (where all the helpers already live):

**`TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession`**
- Setup: git repo, session in ACTIVE_COMMITTED with checkpoint on shadow branch, `PendingCheckpointID` set
- Action: call `HandleTurnEnd` with `[ActionCondense]`
- Assert: `entire/sessions` branch exists, shadow branch deleted, `StepCount` reset, `BaseCommit` updated

**`TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch`**
- Setup: same but corrupt shadow branch to ZeroHash
- Action: call `HandleTurnEnd` with `[ActionCondense]`
- Assert: `BaseCommit` NOT updated, shadow branch preserved

**`TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions`**
- Setup: session in ACTIVE phase (normal turn end)
- Action: call `HandleTurnEnd` with empty actions
- Assert: no-op, state unchanged

### Step 2: Run tests — verify they fail (HandleTurnEnd doesn't exist yet)

### Step 3: Add `TurnEndHandler` interface to `strategy.go`

### Step 4: Implement `HandleTurnEnd` on `ManualCommitStrategy`

In `manual_commit_hooks.go`:

```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState, actions []session.Action) error {
    logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")

    for _, action := range actions {
        switch action {
        case session.ActionCondense:
            // Open repo, derive params from state, condense
            repo, err := OpenRepository()
            if err != nil { return nil /* silent */ }

            head, err := repo.Head()
            if err != nil { return nil }

            checkpointID := id.CheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
            if checkpointID == "" {
                // No pending checkpoint — generate one
                checkpointID = id.NewCheckpointID()
            }

            hasNew, _ := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
            // Fail-open not needed here — if no content, skip condensation

            if hasNew {
                shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
                shadowBranches := map[string]struct{}{}
                s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranches)
                // Delete shadow branches
                for branchName := range shadowBranches {
                    deleteBranch(repo, branchName)
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return nil
}
```

### Step 5: Update `transitionSessionTurnEnd` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`

```go
func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
    // ... existing load/nil checks ...
    result := session.Transition(turnState.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
    remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(turnState, result)

    // Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)
    if len(remaining) > 0 {
        strat := GetStrategy()
        if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
            if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {
                fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
            }
        }
    }

    if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
        fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
    }
}
```

### Step 6: Run tests — all pass

### Step 7: `mise run fmt && mise run lint && mise run test:ci`

### Step 8: Reply to khaong's PR comment with fix commit SHA

## Files Modified

- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go` — new `TurnEndHandler` optional interface
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go` — `HandleTurnEnd` implementation
- `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go` — 3 new test functions
- `cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go` — update `transitionSessionTurnEnd` to dispatch remaining actions

## Key Design Decisions

- **Optional interface** (not on core `Strategy`): follows established pattern, auto-commit doesn't need it
- **Handler receives `[]Action`**: caller doesn't need to know which actions are strategy-specific
- **State saved by caller**: handler mutates state in-place, caller saves — same pattern as PostCommit
- **Shadow branch deletion inside handler**: handler owns the full lifecycle since it knows which branches to clean up
- **`PendingCheckpointID` from state**: set by PostCommit when the commit was made, available for turn-end condensation

   308→// Strategy defines the interface for git operation strategies.
   309→// Different implementations can use commits, branches, stashes, etc.
   310→//
   311→// Note: State capture (tracking untracked files before a session) is handled
   312→// by the CLI layer, not the strategy. The strategy receives pre-computed
   313→// file lists in SaveContext.
   314→type Strategy interface {
   315→	// Name returns the strategy identifier (e.g., "commit", "branch", "stash")
   316→	Name() string
   317→
   318→	// Description returns a human-readable description for the setup wizard
   319→	Description() string
   320→
   321→	// AllowsMainBranch returns true if this strategy can safely operate on the
   322→	// default branch (main/master). Strategies that don't modify the working
   323→	// branch's commit history (like manual-commit strategies) can return true.
   324→	// Strategies that create commits on the working branch should return false
   325→	// to prevent polluting main branch history.
   326→	AllowsMainBranch() bool
   327→
   328→	// ValidateRepository checks if the repository is in a valid state
   329→	// for this strategy to operate. Returns an error if validation fails.

   339→	// - Commit strategy: commits to active branch
   340→	// - Manual-commit strategy: commits to shadow branch
   341→	// - Auto-commit strategy: commits logs to shadow only (code deferred to Stop)
   342→	SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error

   344→	// GetRewindPoints returns available points to rewind to.

   359→	PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error)

   361→	// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   362→	// For strategies that store checkpoints in git (auto-commit), this reads from the branch.
   363→	// For strategies that store checkpoints on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   364→	// Returns nil, nil if not a task checkpoint or checkpoint not found.
   365→	GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error)

   369→	// For strategies that store transcripts on disk (commit, manual-commit), this reads from the filesystem.
   370→	GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error)

   385→	// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata commit for the given checkpoint.
   386→	// Format: "<branch>@<commit-sha>" (e.g., "entire/sessions@abc123").

   388→	GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string

   407→// SessionInitializer is an optional interface for strategies that need to
   408→// initialize session state when a user prompt is submitted.

   411→type SessionInitializer interface {

   416→	// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).

   420→// PrepareCommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to

   428→	//   - "merge": merge commit
   429→	//   - "squash": squash merge
   430→	//   - "commit": amend with -c/-C
   431→	// Should return nil on errors to not block commits (log warnings to stderr).
   432→	PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error

   434→
   435→// PostCommitHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to

   437→type PostCommitHandler interface {
   438→	// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   439→	// Used to perform actions like condensing session data after commits.
   440→	// Should return nil on errors to not block subsequent operations (log warnings to stderr).
   441→	PostCommit() error

   443→
   444→// CommitMsgHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to

   448→	// Used to validate or modify the final commit message before the commit is created.
   449→	// If this returns an error, the commit is aborted.

   453→// PrePushHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to

   455→type PrePushHandler interface {

   457→	// Used to push session branches (e.g., entire/sessions) alongside user pushes.
   458→	// The remote parameter is the name of the remote being pushed to.
   459→	// Should return nil on errors to not block pushes (log warnings to stderr).

   463→// LogsOnlyRestorer is an optional interface for strategies that support
   464→// restoring session logs without file state restoration.
   465→// This is used for "logs-only" rewind points where only the session transcript
   466→// can be restored (file state requires git checkout).
   467→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
   468→	// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   469→	// Does not modify the working directory - only restores the transcript
   470→	// to Claude's project directory.
   471→	// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   472→	RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error

   478→// and session state when a user wants to start fresh.
   479→type SessionResetter interface {

   481→	// Returns nil if there's nothing to reset (no shadow branch).

   489→type ConcurrentSessionChecker interface {

   491→	// with uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.

   499→// that haven't yet been condensed to entire/sessions.

   501→// ListSessions() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks if they
   502→// implement SessionSource, and merges their additional sessions by ID.
   503→type SessionSource interface {
   504→	// GetAdditionalSessions returns sessions not yet on entire/sessions branch.
   505→	GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error)

   512→// ListAllCleanupItems() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks

   514→type OrphanedItemsLister interface {

   517→	ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error)

    27→
    28→// askConfirmTTY prompts the user for a yes/no confirmation via /dev/tty.
    29→// This works even when stdin is redirected (e.g., git commit -m).
    30→// Returns true for yes, false for no. If TTY is unavailable, returns the default.
    31→// If context is non-empty, it is displayed on a separate line before the prompt.
    32→func askConfirmTTY(prompt string, context string, defaultYes bool) bool {
    33→	// Open /dev/tty for both reading and writing
    34→	// This is the controlling terminal, which works even when stdin/stderr are redirected
    35→	tty, err := os.OpenFile("/dev/tty", os.O_RDWR, 0)
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		// Can't open TTY (e.g., running in CI), use default
    38→		return defaultYes
    39→	}
    40→	defer tty.Close()
    41→
    42→	// Show context if provided
    43→	if context != "" {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s\n", context)
    45→	}
    46→
    47→	// Show prompt with default indicator
    48→	// Write to tty directly, not stderr, since git hooks may redirect stderr to /dev/null
    49→	var hint string
    50→	if defaultYes {
    51→		hint = "[Y/n]"
    52→	} else {
    53→		hint = "[y/N]"
    54→	}
    55→	fmt.Fprintf(tty, "%s %s ", prompt, hint)
    56→
    57→	// Read response
    58→	reader := bufio.NewReader(tty)
    59→	response, err := reader.ReadString('\n')
    60→	if err != nil {
    61→		return defaultYes
    62→	}
    63→
    64→	response = strings.TrimSpace(strings.ToLower(response))
    65→	switch response {
    66→	case "y", "yes":
    67→		return true
    68→	case "n", "no":
    69→		return false
    70→	default:
    71→		// Empty or invalid input - use default
    72→		return defaultYes
    73→	}
    74→}
    75→
    76→// CommitMsg is called by the git commit-msg hook after the user edits the message.
    77→// If the message contains only our trailer (no actual user content), strip it
    78→// so git will abort the commit due to empty message.
    79→//
    80→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
    81→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CommitMsg(commitMsgFile string) error {
    82→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from git hook
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	message := string(content)
    88→
    89→	// Check if our trailer is present (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
    90→	if _, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); !found {
    91→		// No trailer, nothing to do
    92→		return nil
    93→	}
    94→
    95→	// Check if there's any user content (non-comment, non-trailer lines)
    96→	if !hasUserContent(message) {
    97→		// No user content - strip the trailer so git aborts
    98→		message = stripCheckpointTrailer(message)
    99→		if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   100→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   101→		}
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	return nil
   105→}
   106→
   107→// hasUserContent checks if the message has any content besides comments and our trailer.
   108→func hasUserContent(message string) bool {
   109→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   110→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   111→		trimmed := strings.TrimSpace(line)
   112→		// Skip empty lines
   113→		if trimmed == "" {
   114→			continue
   115→		}
   116→		// Skip comment lines
   117→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, "#") {
   118→			continue
   119→		}
   120→		// Skip our trailer line
   121→		if strings.HasPrefix(trimmed, trailerPrefix) {
   122→			continue
   123→		}
   124→		// Found user content
   125→		return true
   126→	}
   127→	return false
   128→}
   129→
   130→// stripCheckpointTrailer removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer line from the message.
   131→func stripCheckpointTrailer(message string) string {
   132→	trailerPrefix := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ":"
   133→	var result []string
   134→	for _, line := range strings.Split(message, "\n") {
   135→		if !strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(line), trailerPrefix) {
   136→			result = append(result, line)
   137→		}
   138→	}
   139→	return strings.Join(result, "\n")
   140→}
   141→
   142→// isGitSequenceOperation checks if git is currently in the middle of a rebase,
   143→// cherry-pick, or revert operation. During these operations, commits are being
   144→// replayed and should not be linked to agent sessions.
   145→//
   146→// Detects:
   147→//   - rebase: .git/rebase-merge/ or .git/rebase-apply/ directories
   148→//   - cherry-pick: .git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD file
   149→//   - revert: .git/REVERT_HEAD file
   150→func isGitSequenceOperation() bool {
   151→	// Get git directory (handles worktrees and relative paths correctly)
   152→	gitDir, err := GetGitDir()
   153→	if err != nil {
   154→		return false // Can't determine, assume not in sequence operation
   155→	}
   156→
   157→	// Check for rebase state directories
   158→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")); err == nil {
   159→		return true
   160→	}
   161→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-apply")); err == nil {
   162→		return true
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Check for cherry-pick and revert state files
   166→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "CHERRY_PICK_HEAD")); err == nil {
   167→		return true
   168→	}
   169→	if _, err := os.Stat(filepath.Join(gitDir, "REVERT_HEAD")); err == nil {
   170→		return true
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	return false
   174→}
   175→
   176→// PrepareCommitMsg is called by the git prepare-commit-msg hook.
   177→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer to the commit message with a stable checkpoint ID.
   178→// Only adds a trailer if there's actually new session content to condense.
   179→// The actual condensation happens in PostCommit - if the user removes the trailer,
   180→// the commit will not be linked to the session (useful for "manual" commits).
   181→// For amended commits, preserves the existing checkpoint ID.
   182→//
   183→// The source parameter indicates how the commit was initiated:
   184→//   - "" or "template": normal editor flow - adds trailer with explanatory comment
   185→//   - "message": using -m or -F flag - prompts user interactively via /dev/tty
   186→//   - "merge", "squash": skip trailer entirely (auto-generated messages)
   187→//   - "commit": amend operation - preserves existing trailer or restores from PendingCheckpointID
   188→//
   189→
   190→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PrepareCommitMsg(commitMsgFile string, source string) error {
   191→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   192→
   193→	// Skip during rebase, cherry-pick, or revert operations
   194→	// These are replaying existing commits and should not be linked to agent sessions
   195→	if isGitSequenceOperation() {
   196→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped during git sequence operation",
   197→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   198→			slog.String("source", source),
   199→		)
   200→		return nil
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Skip for merge and squash sources
   204→	// These are auto-generated messages - not from Claude sessions
   205→	switch source {
   206→	case "merge", "squash":
   207→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: skipped for source",
   208→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   209→			slog.String("source", source),
   210→		)
   211→		return nil
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Handle amend (source="commit") separately: preserve or restore trailer
   215→	if source == "commit" {
   216→		return s.handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx, commitMsgFile)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   230→	// We match by worktree (not BaseCommit) because the user may have made
   231→	// intermediate commits without entering new prompts, causing HEAD to diverge
   232→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   233→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   234→		// No active sessions or error listing - silently skip (hooks must be resilient)
   235→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no active sessions",
   236→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   237→			slog.String("source", source),
   238→		)
   239→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	// Check if any session has new content to condense
   243→	sessionsWithContent := s.filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo, sessions)
   244→
   245→	// Determine which checkpoint ID to use
   246→	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
   247→	var hasNewContent bool
   248→	var reusedSession *SessionState
   249→
   250→	if len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   251→		// New content exists - will generate new checkpoint ID below
   252→		hasNewContent = true
   253→	} else {
   254→		// No new content - check if any session has a LastCheckpointID to reuse
   255→		// This handles the case where user splits Claude's work across multiple commits
   256→		// Reuse if: LastCheckpointID exists AND (FilesTouched is empty OR files overlap)
   257→		// - FilesTouched empty: commits made before session stop, reuse checkpoint
   258→		// - FilesTouched populated: only reuse if files overlap (prevents unrelated commits from reusing)
   259→
   260→		// Get current HEAD to filter sessions
   261→		head, err := repo.Head()
   262→		if err != nil {
   263→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   264→		}
   265→		currentHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   266→
   267→		// Filter to sessions where BaseCommit matches current HEAD
   268→		// This prevents reusing checkpoint IDs from old sessions
   269→		// Note: BaseCommit is kept current both when new content is condensed (in the
   270→		// condensation process) and when no new content is found (via PostCommit when
   271→		// reusing checkpoint IDs). If none match, we don't add a trailer rather than
   272→		// falling back to old sessions which could have stale checkpoint IDs.
   273→		var currentSessions []*SessionState
   274→		for _, session := range sessions {
   275→			if session.BaseCommit == currentHeadHash {
   276→				currentSessions = append(currentSessions, session)
   277→			}
   278→		}
   279→
   280→		if len(currentSessions) == 0 {
   281→			// No sessions match current HEAD - don't try to reuse checkpoint IDs
   282→			// from old sessions as they may be stale
   283→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no sessions match current HEAD",
   284→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   285→				slog.String("source", source),
   286→				slog.String("current_head", currentHeadHash[:7]),
   287→				slog.Int("total_sessions", len(sessions)),
   288→			)
   289→			return nil
   290→		}
   291→
   292→		stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo)
   293→		for _, session := range currentSessions {
   294→			if !session.LastCheckpointID.IsEmpty() &&
   295→				(len(session.FilesTouched) == 0 || hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, session.FilesTouched)) {
   296→				checkpointID = session.LastCheckpointID
   297→				reusedSession = session
   298→				break
   299→			}
   300→		}
   301→		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   302→			// No new content and no previous checkpoint to reference (or staged files are unrelated)
   303→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: no content to link",
   304→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   305→				slog.String("source", source),
   306→				slog.Int("sessions_found", len(sessions)),
   307→				slog.Int("sessions_with_content", len(sessionsWithContent)),
   308→			)
   309→			return nil
   310→		}
   311→	}
   312→
   313→	// Read current commit message
   314→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	message := string(content)
   320→
   321→	// Get or generate checkpoint ID (ParseCheckpoint validates format, so found==true means valid)
   322→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   323→		// Trailer already exists (e.g., amend) - keep it
   324→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer already exists",
   325→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   326→			slog.String("source", source),
   327→			slog.String("existing_checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   328→		)
   329→		return nil
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	if hasNewContent {
   333→		// New content: check PendingCheckpointID first (set during previous condensation),
   334→		// otherwise generate a new one. This ensures idempotent IDs across hook invocations.
   335→		for _, state := range sessionsWithContent {
   336→			if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
   337→				if cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); err == nil {
   338→					checkpointID = cpID
   339→					break
   340→				}
   341→			}
   342→		}
   343→		if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   344→			cpID, err := id.Generate()
   345→			if err != nil {
   346→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   347→			}
   348→			checkpointID = cpID
   349→		}
   350→	}
   351→	// Otherwise checkpointID is already set to LastCheckpointID from above
   352→
   353→	// Determine agent type and last prompt from session
   354→	agentType := DefaultAgentType // default for backward compatibility
   355→	var lastPrompt string
   356→	if hasNewContent && len(sessionsWithContent) > 0 {
   357→		session := sessionsWithContent[0]
   358→		if session.AgentType != "" {
   359→			agentType = session.AgentType
   360→		}
   361→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, session)
   362→	} else if reusedSession != nil {
   363→		// Reusing checkpoint from existing session - get agent type and prompt from that session
   364→		if reusedSession.AgentType != "" {
   365→			agentType = reusedSession.AgentType
   366→		}
   367→		lastPrompt = s.getLastPrompt(repo, reusedSession)
   368→	}
   369→
   370→	// Prepare prompt for display: collapse newlines/whitespace, then truncate (rune-safe)
   371→	displayPrompt := stringutil.TruncateRunes(stringutil.CollapseWhitespace(lastPrompt), 80, "...")
   372→
   373→	// Add trailer differently based on commit source
   374→	if source == "message" {
   375→		// Using -m or -F: ask user interactively whether to add trailer
   376→		// (comments won't be stripped by git in this mode)
   377→
   378→		// Build context string for interactive prompt
   379→		var promptContext string
   380→		if displayPrompt != "" {
   381→			promptContext = "You have an active " + string(agentType) + " session.\nLast Prompt: " + displayPrompt
   382→		}
   383→
   384→		if !askConfirmTTY("Link this commit to "+string(agentType)+" session context?", promptContext, true) {
   385→			// User declined - don't add trailer
   386→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: user declined trailer",
   387→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   388→				slog.String("source", source),
   389→			)
   390→			return nil
   391→		}
   392→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, checkpointID)
   393→	} else {
   394→		// Normal editor flow: add trailer with explanatory comment (will be stripped by git)
   395→		message = addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message, checkpointID, string(agentType), displayPrompt)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: trailer added",
   399→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   400→		slog.String("source", source),
   401→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   402→		slog.Bool("has_new_content", hasNewContent),
   403→	)
   404→
   405→	// Write updated message back
   406→	if err := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); err != nil {
   407→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	return nil
   411→}
   412→
   413→// handleAmendCommitMsg handles the prepare-commit-msg hook for amend operations
   414→// (source="commit"). It preserves existing trailers or restores from PendingCheckpointID.
   415→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleAmendCommitMsg(logCtx context.Context, commitMsgFile string) error {
   416→	// Read current commit message
   417→	content, err := os.ReadFile(commitMsgFile) //nolint:gosec // commitMsgFile is provided by git hook
   418→	if err != nil {
   419→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   420→	}
   421→
   422→	message := string(content)
   423→
   424→	// If message already has a trailer, keep it unchanged
   425→	if existingCpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(message); found {
   426→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend preserves existing trailer",
   427→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   428→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", existingCpID.String()),
   429→		)
   430→		return nil
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	// No trailer in message — check if any session has PendingCheckpointID to restore
   434→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   435→	if err != nil {
   436→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   437→	}
   438→
   439→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   440→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   441→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions - nothing to restore
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Find first session with PendingCheckpointID
   445→	for _, state := range sessions {
   446→		if state.PendingCheckpointID == "" {
   447→			continue
   448→		}
   449→
   450→		cpID, err := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID)
   451→		if err != nil {
   452→			continue // Invalid ID, skip
   453→		}
   454→
   455→		// Restore the trailer
   456→		message = addCheckpointTrailer(message, cpID)
   457→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(commitMsgFile, []byte(message), 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   458→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   459→		}
   460→
   461→		logging.Info(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: restored trailer on amend from PendingCheckpointID",
   462→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   463→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   464→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   465→		)
   466→		return nil
   467→	}
   468→
   469→	// No PendingCheckpointID found - leave message unchanged
   470→	logging.Debug(logCtx, "prepare-commit-msg: amend with no pending checkpoint to restore",
   471→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   472→	)
   473→	return nil
   474→}
   475→
   476→// PostCommit is called by the git post-commit hook after a commit is created.
   477→// Uses the session state machine to determine what action to take per session:
   478→//   - ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED: defer condensation (agent still working)
   479→//   - IDLE → condense immediately
   480→//   - ACTIVE_COMMITTED → migrate shadow branch (additional commit during same turn)
   481→//   - ENDED → condense if files touched, discard if empty
   482→//
   483→// Shadow branches are only deleted when ALL sessions sharing the branch are non-active.
   484→// During rebase/cherry-pick/revert operations, phase transitions are skipped entirely.
   485→//
   486→//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
   487→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PostCommit() error {
   488→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   489→
   490→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   491→	if err != nil {
   492→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   493→	}
   494→
   495→	// Get HEAD commit to check for trailer
   496→	head, err := repo.Head()
   497→	if err != nil {
   498→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   502→	if err != nil {
   503→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   504→	}
   505→
   506→	// Check if commit has checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   507→	checkpointID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(commit.Message)
   508→	if !found {
   509→		// No trailer - user removed it, treat as manual commit
   510→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: no checkpoint trailer",
   511→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   512→		)
   513→		return nil
   514→	}
   515→
   516→	worktreePath, err := GetWorktreePath()
   517→	if err != nil {
   518→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Hook must be silent on failure
   519→	}
   520→
   521→	// Find all active sessions for this worktree
   522→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForWorktree(worktreePath)
   523→	if err != nil || len(sessions) == 0 {
   524→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: no active sessions despite trailer",
   525→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   526→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   527→		)
   528→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: hooks must be silent on failure
   529→	}
   530→
   531→	// Build transition context
   532→	isRebase := isGitSequenceOperation()
   533→	transitionCtx := session.TransitionContext{
   534→		IsRebaseInProgress: isRebase,
   535→	}
   536→
   537→	if isRebase {
   538→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: rebase/sequence in progress, skipping phase transitions",
   539→			slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   540→		)
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	// Track shadow branch names and whether they can be deleted
   544→	shadowBranchesToDelete := make(map[string]struct{})
   545→	// Track sessions that are still active AFTER transitions
   546→	activeSessionsOnBranch := make(map[string]bool)
   547→
   548→	newHead := head.Hash().String()
   549→
   550→	// Two-pass processing: condensation first, migration second.
   551→	// This prevents a migration from renaming a shadow branch before another
   552→	// session sharing that branch has had a chance to condense from it.
   553→	type pendingMigration struct {
   554→		state *SessionState
   555→	}
   556→	var pendingMigrations []pendingMigration
   557→
   558→	// Pass 1: Run transitions and dispatch condensation/discard actions.
   559→	// Defer migration actions to pass 2.
   560→	for _, state := range sessions {
   561→		shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   562→
   563→		// Check for new content (needed for TransitionContext and condensation).
   564→		// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content exists so we
   565→		// don't silently skip data that should have been condensed.
   566→		hasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   567→		if contentErr != nil {
   568→			hasNew = true
   569→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: error checking session content, assuming new content",
   570→				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   571→				slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()),
   572→			)
   573→		}
   574→		transitionCtx.HasFilesTouched = len(state.FilesTouched) > 0
   575→
   576→		// Run the state machine transition
   577→		result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventGitCommit, transitionCtx)
   578→		remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   579→
   580→		// Dispatch strategy-specific actions.
   581→		// Each branch handles its own BaseCommit update so there is no
   582→		// fallthrough conditional at the end. On condensation failure,
   583→		// BaseCommit is intentionally NOT updated to preserve access to
   584→		// the shadow branch (which is named after the old BaseCommit).
   585→		for _, action := range remaining {
   586→			switch action {
   587→			case session.ActionCondense:
   588→				if hasNew {
   589→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   590→					// condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on success.
   591→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved so the shadow branch remains accessible.
   592→				} else {
   593→					// No new content to condense — just update BaseCommit
   594→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   595→				}
   596→			case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched:
   597→				// The state machine already gates this action on HasFilesTouched,
   598→				// but hasNew is an additional content-level check (transcript has
   599→				// new content beyond what was previously condensed).
   600→				if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 && hasNew {
   601→					s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
   602→					// On failure, BaseCommit is preserved (same as ActionCondense).
   603→				} else {
   604→					s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   605→				}
   606→			case session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles:
   607→				if len(state.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   608→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: skipping empty ended session (no files to condense)",
   609→						slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   610→					)
   611→				}
   612→				s.updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx, state, newHead)
   613→			case session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch:
   614→				// Deferred to pass 2 so condensation reads the old shadow branch first.
   615→				// Migration updates BaseCommit as part of the rename.
   616→				pendingMigrations = append(pendingMigrations, pendingMigration{state: state})
   617→			case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   618→				// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions above
   619→			case session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
   620→				// Not produced by EventGitCommit; listed for switch exhaustiveness
   621→			}
   622→		}
   623→
   624→		// Save the updated state
   625→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   626→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", err)
   627→		}
   628→
   629→		// Track whether any session on this shadow branch is still active
   630→		if state.Phase.IsActive() {
   631→			activeSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] = true
   632→		}
   633→	}
   634→
   635→	// Pass 2: Run deferred migrations now that all condensations are complete.
   636→	for _, pm := range pendingMigrations {
   637→		if _, migErr := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, pm.state); migErr != nil {
   638→			logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: shadow branch migration failed",
   639→				slog.String("session_id", pm.state.SessionID),
   640→				slog.String("error", migErr.Error()),
   641→			)
   642→		}
   643→		// Save the migrated state
   644→		if err := s.saveSessionState(pm.state); err != nil {
   645→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to update session state after migration: %v\n", err)
   646→		}
   647→	}
   648→
   649→	// Clean up shadow branches — only delete when ALL sessions on the branch are non-active
   650→	for shadowBranchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
   651→		if activeSessionsOnBranch[shadowBranchName] {
   652→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: preserving shadow branch (active session exists)",
   653→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   654→			)
   655→			continue
   656→		}
   657→		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   658→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", shadowBranchName, err)
   659→		} else {
   660→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", shadowBranchName)
   661→			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted",
   662→				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   663→				slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   664→			)
   665→		}
   666→	}
   667→
   668→	return nil
   669→}
   670→
   671→// condenseAndUpdateState runs condensation for a session and updates state afterward.
   672→// Returns true if condensation succeeded.
   673→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) condenseAndUpdateState(
   674→	logCtx context.Context,
   675→	repo *git.Repository,
   676→	checkpointID id.CheckpointID,
   677→	state *SessionState,
   678→	head *plumbing.Reference,
   679→	shadowBranchName string,
   680→	shadowBranchesToDelete map[string]struct{},
   681→) {
   682→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   683→	if err != nil {
   684→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: condensation failed for session %s: %v\n",
   685→			state.SessionID, err)
   686→		logging.Warn(logCtx, "post-commit: condensation failed",
   687→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   688→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   689→		)
   690→		return
   691→	}
   692→
   693→	// Track this shadow branch for cleanup
   694→	shadowBranchesToDelete[shadowBranchName] = struct{}{}
   695→
   696→	// Update session state for the new base commit
   697→	state.BaseCommit = head.Hash().String()
   698→	state.StepCount = 0
   699→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   700→
   701→	// Clear attribution tracking — condensation already used these values
   702→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   703→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   704→
   705→	// Save checkpoint ID so subsequent commits can reuse it
   706→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   707→	// Set PendingCheckpointID for amend trailer restoration
   708→	state.PendingCheckpointID = checkpointID.String()
   709→
   710→	shortID := state.SessionID
   711→	if len(shortID) > 8 {
   712→		shortID = shortID[:8]
   713→	}
   714→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Condensed session %s: %s (%d checkpoints)\n",
   715→		shortID, result.CheckpointID, result.CheckpointsCount)
   716→
   717→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session condensed",
   718→		slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
   719→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   720→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   721→		slog.Int("transcript_lines", result.TotalTranscriptLines),
   722→	)
   723→}
   724→
   725→// updateBaseCommitIfChanged updates BaseCommit to newHead if it changed.
   726→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) updateBaseCommitIfChanged(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState, newHead string) {
   727→	if state.BaseCommit != newHead {
   728→		state.BaseCommit = newHead
   729→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "post-commit: updated BaseCommit",
   730→			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   731→			slog.String("new_head", newHead[:7]),
   732→		)
   733→	}
   734→}
   735→
   736→// filterSessionsWithNewContent returns sessions that have new transcript content
   737→// beyond what was already condensed.
   738→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) filterSessionsWithNewContent(repo *git.Repository, sessions []*SessionState) []*SessionState {
   739→	var result []*SessionState
   740→
   741→	for _, state := range sessions {
   742→		hasNew, err := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
   743→		if err != nil {
   744→			// On error, include the session (fail open for hooks)
   745→			result = append(result, state)
   746→			continue
   747→		}
   748→		if hasNew {
   749→			result = append(result, state)
   750→		}
   751→	}
   752→
   753→	return result
   754→}
   755→
   756→// sessionHasNewContent checks if a session has new transcript content
   757→// beyond what was already condensed.
   758→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContent(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   759→	// Get shadow branch
   760→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   761→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   762→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   763→	if err != nil {
   764→		// No shadow branch means no Stop has happened since the last condensation.
   765→		// However, the agent may have done work (including commits) without a Stop.
   766→		// Check the live transcript to detect this scenario.
   767→		return s.sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo, state)
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   776→	if err != nil {
   777→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   778→	}
   779→
   780→	// Look for transcript file
   781→	metadataDir := paths.EntireMetadataDir + "/" + state.SessionID
   782→	var transcriptLines int
   783→
   784→	if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   785→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   786→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   787→		}
   788→	} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   789→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   790→			transcriptLines = countTranscriptLines(content)
   791→		}
   792→	}
   793→
   794→	// Has new content if there are more lines than already condensed
   795→	return transcriptLines > state.CheckpointTranscriptStart, nil
   796→}
   797→
   798→// countTranscriptLines counts lines in a transcript, matching the counting method used
   799→// in extractSessionData for consistency. This trims trailing empty lines (from final \n
   800→// in JSONL) but includes empty lines in the middle of the file.
   801→func countTranscriptLines(content string) int {
   802→	lines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   803→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   804→	for len(lines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(lines[len(lines)-1]) == "" {
   805→		lines = lines[:len(lines)-1]
   806→	}
   807→	return len(lines)
   808→}
   809→
   810→// sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript checks if a session has new content
   811→// by examining the live transcript file. This is used when no shadow branch exists
   812→// (i.e., no Stop has happened yet) but the agent may have done work.
   813→//
   814→// Returns true if:
   815→//  1. The transcript has grown since the last condensation, AND
   816→//  2. The new transcript portion contains file modifications, AND
   817→//  3. At least one modified file overlaps with the currently staged files
   818→//
   819→// The overlap check ensures we don't add checkpoint trailers to commits that are
   820→// unrelated to the agent's recent changes.
   821→//
   822→// This handles the scenario where the agent commits mid-session before Stop.
   823→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) sessionHasNewContentFromLiveTranscript(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) (bool, error) {
   824→	// Need both transcript path and agent type to analyze
   825→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" || state.AgentType == "" {
   826→		return false, nil
   827→	}
   828→
   829→	// Get the agent for transcript analysis
   830→	ag, err := agent.GetByAgentType(state.AgentType)
   831→	if err != nil {
   832→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Unknown agent type, fail gracefully
   833→	}
   834→
   835→	// Cast to TranscriptAnalyzer
   836→	analyzer, ok := ag.(agent.TranscriptAnalyzer)
   837→	if !ok {
   838→		return false, nil // Agent doesn't support transcript analysis
   839→	}
   840→
   841→	// Get current transcript position
   842→	currentPos, err := analyzer.GetTranscriptPosition(state.TranscriptPath)
   843→	if err != nil {
   844→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error reading transcript, fail gracefully
   845→	}
   846→
   847→	// Check if transcript has grown since last condensation
   848→	if currentPos <= state.CheckpointTranscriptStart {
   849→		return false, nil // No new content
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Transcript has grown - check if there are file modifications in the new portion
   853→	modifiedFiles, _, err := analyzer.ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   854→	if err != nil {
   855→		return false, nil //nolint:nilerr // Error parsing transcript, fail gracefully
   856→	}
   857→
   858→	// No file modifications means no new content to checkpoint
   859→	if len(modifiedFiles) == 0 {
   860→		return false, nil
   861→	}
   862→
   863→	// Check if any modified files overlap with currently staged files
   864→	// This ensures we only add checkpoint trailers to commits that include
   865→	// files the agent actually modified
   866→	stagedFiles := getStagedFiles(repo) // TODO: does it work? Do we have staged files if this is called at post-commit hook ?
   867→	if !hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, modifiedFiles) {
   868→		return false, nil // No overlap - staged files are unrelated to agent's work
   869→	}
   870→
   871→	return true, nil
   872→}
   873→
   874→// addCheckpointTrailer adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer to a commit message.
   875→// Handles proper trailer formatting (blank line before trailers if needed).
   876→func addCheckpointTrailer(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) string {
   877→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   878→
   879→	// If message already ends with trailers (lines starting with key:), just append
   880→	// Otherwise, add a blank line first
   881→	lines := strings.Split(strings.TrimRight(message, "\n"), "\n")
   882→
   883→	// Check if last non-empty, non-comment line looks like a trailer
   884→	// Git comment lines start with # and may contain ": " (e.g., "# Changes to be committed:")
   885→	hasTrailers := false
   886→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   887→		line := strings.TrimSpace(lines[i])
   888→		if line == "" {
   889→			break
   890→		}
   891→		// Skip git comment lines
   892→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   893→			continue
   894→		}
   895→		if strings.Contains(line, ": ") {
   896→			hasTrailers = true
   897→			break
   898→		}
   899→		// Non-comment, non-trailer line found - no existing trailers
   900→		break
   901→	}
   902→
   903→	if hasTrailers {
   904→		// Append trailer directly
   905→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n" + trailer + "\n"
   906→	}
   907→
   908→	// Add blank line before trailer
   909→	return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n"
   910→}
   911→
   912→// addCheckpointTrailerWithComment adds the Entire-Checkpoint trailer with an explanatory comment.
   913→// The trailer is placed above the git comment block but below the user's message area,
   914→// with a comment explaining that the user can remove it if they don't want to link the commit
   915→// to the agent session. If prompt is non-empty, it's shown as context.
   916→func addCheckpointTrailerWithComment(message string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, agentName, prompt string) string {
   917→	trailer := trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   918→	commentLines := []string{
   919→		"# Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer above if you don't want to link this commit to " + agentName + " session context.",
   920→	}
   921→	if prompt != "" {
   922→		commentLines = append(commentLines, "# Last Prompt: "+prompt)
   923→	}
   924→	commentLines = append(commentLines, "# The trailer will be added to your next commit based on this branch.")
   925→	comment := strings.Join(commentLines, "\n")
   926→
   927→	lines := strings.Split(message, "\n")
   928→
   929→	// Find where the git comment block starts (first # line)
   930→	commentStart := -1
   931→	for i, line := range lines {
   932→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "#") {
   933→			commentStart = i
   934→			break
   935→		}
   936→	}
   937→
   938→	if commentStart == -1 {
   939→		// No git comments, append trailer at the end
   940→		return strings.TrimRight(message, "\n") + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n"
   941→	}
   942→
   943→	// Split into user content and git comments
   944→	userContent := strings.Join(lines[:commentStart], "\n")
   945→	gitComments := strings.Join(lines[commentStart:], "\n")
   946→
   947→	// Build result: user content, blank line, trailer, comment, blank line, git comments
   948→	userContent = strings.TrimRight(userContent, "\n")
   949→	if userContent == "" {
   950→		// No user content yet - leave space for them to type, then trailer
   951→		// Two newlines: first for user's message line, second for blank separator
   952→		return "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   953→	}
   954→	return userContent + "\n\n" + trailer + "\n" + comment + "\n\n" + gitComments
   955→}
   956→
   957→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session or updates an existing one.
   958→// This implements the optional SessionInitializer interface.
   959→// Called during UserPromptSubmit to allow git hooks to detect active sessions.
   960→//
   961→// If the session already exists and HEAD has moved (e.g., user committed), updates
   962→// BaseCommit to the new HEAD so future checkpoints go to the correct shadow branch.
   963→//
   964→// If there's an existing shadow branch with commits from a different session ID,
   965→// returns a SessionIDConflictError to prevent orphaning existing session work.
   966→//
   967→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   968→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   969→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   970→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   971→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   972→	if err != nil {
   973→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   974→	}
   975→
   976→	// Check if session already exists
   977→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   978→	if err != nil {
   979→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check session state: %w", err)
   980→	}
   981→
   982→	if state != nil && state.BaseCommit != "" {
   983→		// Session is fully initialized — apply phase transition for TurnStart
   984→		result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{})
   985→		session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
   986→
   987→		// Backfill AgentType if empty or set to the generic default "Agent"
   988→		if !isSpecificAgentType(state.AgentType) && agentType != "" {
   989→			state.AgentType = agentType
   990→		}
   991→
   992→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions created before the first_prompt field was added)
   993→		if state.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
   994→			state.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
   995→		}
   996→
   997→		// Update transcript path if provided (may change on session resume)
   998→		if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
   999→			state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
  1000→		}
  1001→
  1002→		// Clear checkpoint IDs on every new prompt
  1003→		// These are set during PostCommit when a checkpoint is created, and should be
  1004→		// cleared when the user enters a new prompt (starting fresh work)
  1005→		if state.LastCheckpointID != "" {
  1006→			state.LastCheckpointID = ""
  1007→		}
  1008→		if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
  1009→			state.PendingCheckpointID = ""
  1010→		}
  1011→
  1012→		// Calculate attribution at prompt start (BEFORE agent makes any changes)
  1013→		// This captures user edits since the last checkpoint (or base commit for first prompt).
  1014→		// IMPORTANT: Always calculate attribution, even for the first checkpoint, to capture
  1015→		// user edits made before the first prompt. The inner CalculatePromptAttribution handles
  1016→		// nil lastCheckpointTree by falling back to baseTree.
  1017→		promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1018→		state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1019→
  1020→		// Check if HEAD has moved (user pulled/rebased or committed)
  1021→		// migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded handles renaming the shadow branch and updating state.BaseCommit
  1022→		if _, err := s.migrateShadowBranchIfNeeded(repo, state); err != nil {
  1023→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check/migrate shadow branch: %w", err)
  1024→		}
  1025→
  1026→		if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1027→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
  1028→		}
  1029→		return nil
  1030→	}
  1031→	// If state exists but BaseCommit is empty, it's a partial state from concurrent warning
  1032→	// Continue below to properly initialize it
  1033→
  1034→	// Initialize new session
  1035→	state, err = s.initializeSession(repo, sessionID, agentType, transcriptPath, userPrompt)
  1036→	if err != nil {
  1037→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to initialize session: %w", err)
  1038→	}
  1039→
  1040→	// Apply phase transition: new session starts as ACTIVE
  1041→	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnStart, session.TransitionContext{})
  1042→	session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
  1043→
  1044→	// Calculate attribution for pre-prompt edits
  1045→	// This captures any user edits made before the first prompt
  1046→	promptAttr := s.calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(repo, state)
  1047→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = &promptAttr
  1048→	if err = s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
  1049→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save attribution: %w", err)
  1050→	}
  1051→
  1052→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Initialized shadow session: %s\n", sessionID)
  1053→	return nil
  1054→}
  1055→
  1056→// calculatePromptAttributionAtStart calculates attribution at prompt start (before agent runs).
  1057→// This captures user changes since the last checkpoint - no filtering needed since
  1058→// the agent hasn't made any changes yet.
  1059→//
  1060→// IMPORTANT: This reads from the worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1061→// captures in checkpoints. If we read staged content but checkpoints capture worktree content,
  1062→// unstaged changes would be in the checkpoint but not counted in PromptAttribution, causing
  1063→// them to be incorrectly attributed to the agent later.
  1064→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) calculatePromptAttributionAtStart(
  1065→	repo *git.Repository,
  1066→	state *SessionState,
  1067→) PromptAttribution {
  1068→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
  1069→	nextCheckpointNum := state.StepCount + 1
  1070→	result := PromptAttribution{CheckpointNumber: nextCheckpointNum}
  1071→
  1072→	// Get last checkpoint tree from shadow branch (if it exists)
  1073→	// For the first checkpoint, no shadow branch exists yet - this is fine,
  1074→	// CalculatePromptAttribution will use baseTree as the reference instead.
  1075→	var lastCheckpointTree *object.Tree
  1076→	shadowBranchName := checkpoint.ShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1077→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1078→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1079→	if err != nil {
  1080→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: no shadow branch yet (first checkpoint)",
  1081→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName))
  1082→		// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1083→	} else {
  1084→		shadowCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1085→		if err != nil {
  1086→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow commit",
  1087→				slog.String("shadow_ref", ref.Hash().String()),
  1088→				slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1089→			// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1090→		} else {
  1091→			lastCheckpointTree, err = shadowCommit.Tree()
  1092→			if err != nil {
  1093→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: failed to get shadow tree",
  1094→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1095→				// Continue with lastCheckpointTree = nil
  1096→			}
  1097→		}
  1098→	}
  1099→
  1100→	// Get base tree for agent lines calculation
  1101→	var baseTree *object.Tree
  1102→	if baseCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(state.BaseCommit)); err == nil {
  1103→		if tree, treeErr := baseCommit.Tree(); treeErr == nil {
  1104→			baseTree = tree
  1105→		} else {
  1106→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base tree unavailable",
  1107→				slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
  1108→		}
  1109→	} else {
  1110→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution: base commit unavailable",
  1111→			slog.String("base_commit", state.BaseCommit),
  1112→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1113→	}
  1114→
  1115→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1116→	if err != nil {
  1117→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree",
  1118→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1119→		return result
  1120→	}
  1121→
  1122→	// Get worktree status to find ALL changed files
  1123→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1124→	if err != nil {
  1125→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution skipped: failed to get worktree status",
  1126→			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
  1127→		return result
  1128→	}
  1129→
  1130→	worktreeRoot := worktree.Filesystem.Root()
  1131→
  1132→	// Build map of changed files with their worktree content
  1133→	// IMPORTANT: We read from worktree (not staging area) to match what WriteTemporary
  1134→	// captures in checkpoints. This ensures attribution is consistent.
  1135→	changedFiles := make(map[string]string)
  1136→	for filePath, fileStatus := range status {
  1137→		// Skip unmodified files
  1138→		if fileStatus.Worktree == git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging == git.Unmodified {
  1139→			continue
  1140→		}
  1141→		// Skip .entire metadata directory (session data, not user code)
  1142→		if strings.HasPrefix(filePath, paths.EntireMetadataDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(filePath, ".entire/") {
  1143→			continue
  1144→		}
  1145→
  1146→		// Always read from worktree to match checkpoint behavior
  1147→		fullPath := filepath.Join(worktreeRoot, filePath)
  1148→		var content string
  1149→		if data, err := os.ReadFile(fullPath); err == nil { //nolint:gosec // filePath is from git worktree status
  1150→			// Use git's binary detection algorithm (matches getFileContent behavior).
  1151→			// Binary files are excluded from line-based attribution calculations.
  1152→			isBinary, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(data))
  1153→			if binErr == nil && !isBinary {
  1154→				content = string(data)
  1155→			}
  1156→		}
  1157→		// else: file deleted, unreadable, or binary - content remains empty string
  1158→
  1159→		changedFiles[filePath] = content
  1160→	}
  1161→
  1162→	// Use CalculatePromptAttribution from manual_commit_attribution.go
  1163→	result = CalculatePromptAttribution(baseTree, lastCheckpointTree, changedFiles, nextCheckpointNum)
  1164→
  1165→	return result
  1166→}
  1167→
  1168→// getStagedFiles returns a list of files staged for commit.
  1169→func getStagedFiles(repo *git.Repository) []string {
  1170→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1171→	if err != nil {
  1172→		return nil
  1173→	}
  1174→
  1175→	status, err := worktree.Status()
  1176→	if err != nil {
  1177→		return nil
  1178→	}
  1179→
  1180→	var staged []string
  1181→	for path, fileStatus := range status {
  1182→		// Check if file is staged (in index)
  1183→		if fileStatus.Staging != git.Unmodified && fileStatus.Staging != git.Untracked {
  1184→			staged = append(staged, path)
  1185→		}
  1186→	}
  1187→	return staged
  1188→}
  1189→
  1190→// getLastPrompt retrieves the most recent user prompt from a session's shadow branch.
  1191→// Returns empty string if no prompt can be retrieved.
  1192→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getLastPrompt(repo *git.Repository, state *SessionState) string {
  1193→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
  1194→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
  1195→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1196→	if err != nil {
  1197→		return ""
  1198→	}
  1199→
  1200→	// Extract session data to get prompts for commit message generation
  1201→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini)
  1202→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, nil, state.AgentType)
  1203→	if err != nil || len(sessionData.Prompts) == 0 {
  1204→		return ""
  1205→	}
  1206→
  1207→	// Return the last prompt (most recent work before commit)
  1208→	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]
  1209→}
  1210→
  1211→// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.
  1212→func hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {
  1213→	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1214→	for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1215→		touchedSet[f] = true
  1216→	}
  1217→
  1218→	for _, staged := range stagedFiles {
  1219→		if touchedSet[staged] {
  1220→			return true
  1221→		}
  1222→	}
  1223→	return false
  1224→}

    21→// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation verifies that PostCommit on an
    22→// ACTIVE session transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED without condensing.
    23→// The shadow branch must be preserved because the session is still active.
    24→func TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_NoCondensation(t *testing.T) {
    25→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    26→	t.Chdir(dir)
    27→
    28→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    29→	require.NoError(t, err)
    30→
    31→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    32→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-active"
    33→
    34→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    35→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    36→
    37→	// Set phase to ACTIVE (simulating agent mid-turn)
    38→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    39→	require.NoError(t, err)
    40→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
    41→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    42→
    43→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer on the main branch
    44→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
    45→
    46→	// Run PostCommit
    47→	err = s.PostCommit()
    48→	require.NoError(t, err)
    49→
    50→	// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
    51→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    52→	require.NoError(t, err)
    53→	require.NotNil(t, state)
    54→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
    55→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
    56→
    57→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted (session is still active).
    58→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD, so use the current state.
    59→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    60→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
    61→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
    62→	assert.NoError(t, err,
    63→		"shadow branch should be preserved when session is still active")
    64→}
    65→
    66→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses verifies that PostCommit on an IDLE
    67→// session condenses session data and cleans up the shadow branch.
    68→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
    69→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
    70→	t.Chdir(dir)
    71→
    72→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
    73→	require.NoError(t, err)
    74→
    75→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
    76→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle"
    77→
    78→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
    79→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
    80→
    81→	// Set phase to IDLE (agent turn finished, waiting for user)
    82→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
    83→	require.NoError(t, err)
    84→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
    85→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
    86→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
    87→
    88→	// Record shadow branch name before PostCommit
    89→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
    90→
    91→	// Create a commit WITH the Entire-Checkpoint trailer
    92→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
    93→
    94→	// Run PostCommit
    95→	err = s.PostCommit()
    96→	require.NoError(t, err)
    97→
    98→	// Verify condensation happened: the entire/sessions branch should exist
    99→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   100→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   101→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   102→
   103→	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
   104→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   105→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   106→	assert.Error(t, err,
   107→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for IDLE session")
   108→}
