```
   110→// TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition verifies that PostCommit
   111→// is a no-op during rebase operations, leaving the session phase unchanged.
   112→func TestPostCommit_RebaseDuringActive_SkipsTransition(t *testing.T) {
   113→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   114→	t.Chdir(dir)
   115→
   116→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   117→	require.NoError(t, err)
   118→
   119→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   120→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-rebase"
   121→
   122→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   123→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   124→
   125→	// Set phase to ACTIVE
   126→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   127→	require.NoError(t, err)
   128→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
   129→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   130→
   131→	// Capture shadow branch name BEFORE any state changes
   132→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   133→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   134→
   135→	// Simulate rebase in progress by creating .git/rebase-merge/ directory
   136→	gitDir := filepath.Join(dir, ".git")
   137→	rebaseMergeDir := filepath.Join(gitDir, "rebase-merge")
   138→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(rebaseMergeDir, 0o755))
   139→	defer os.RemoveAll(rebaseMergeDir)
   140→
   141→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   142→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "c3d4e5f6a1b2")
   143→
   144→	// Run PostCommit
   145→	err = s.PostCommit()
   146→	require.NoError(t, err)
   147→
   148→	// Verify phase stayed ACTIVE (no transition during rebase)
   149→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   150→	require.NoError(t, err)
   151→	require.NotNil(t, state)
   152→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state.Phase,
   153→		"session should stay ACTIVE during rebase (no transition)")
   154→
   155→	// Verify StepCount was NOT reset (no condensation happened)
   156→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   157→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation during rebase")
   158→
   159→	// Verify NO condensation happened (entire/sessions branch should not exist)
   160→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   161→	require.Error(t, err,
   162→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation during rebase")
   163→
   164→	// Verify shadow branch still exists (not cleaned up during rebase)
   165→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   166→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   167→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   168→		"shadow branch should be preserved during rebase")
   169→}
   170→
   171→// TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists verifies that
   172→// the shadow branch is preserved when ANY session on it is still active,
   173→// even if another session on the same branch is IDLE and gets condensed.
   174→func TestPostCommit_ShadowBranch_PreservedWhenActiveSessionExists(t *testing.T) {
   175→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   176→	t.Chdir(dir)
   177→
   178→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   179→	require.NoError(t, err)
   180→
   181→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   182→	idleSessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-multi"
   183→	activeSessionID := "test-postcommit-active-multi"
   184→
   185→	// Initialize the idle session with a checkpoint
   186→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, idleSessionID)
   187→
   188→	// Get worktree path and base commit from the idle session
   189→	idleState, err := s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   190→	require.NoError(t, err)
   191→	worktreePath := idleState.WorktreePath
   192→	baseCommit := idleState.BaseCommit
   193→	worktreeID := idleState.WorktreeID
   194→
   195→	// Set idle session to IDLE phase
   196→	idleState.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   197→	idleState.LastInteractionTime = nil
   198→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(idleState))
   199→
   200→	// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent session)
   201→	// Save the active session with ACTIVE phase and some checkpoints
   202→	now := time.Now()
   203→	activeState := &SessionState{
   204→		SessionID:           activeSessionID,
   205→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   206→		WorktreePath:        worktreePath,
   207→		WorktreeID:          worktreeID,
   208→		StartedAt:           now,
   209→		Phase:               session.PhaseActive,
   210→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   211→		StepCount:           1,
   212→	}
   213→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(activeState))
   214→
   215→	// Create a commit WITH the checkpoint trailer
   216→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c3")
   217→
   218→	// Run PostCommit
   219→	err = s.PostCommit()
   220→	require.NoError(t, err)
   221→
   222→	// Verify the ACTIVE session's phase is now ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   223→	activeState, err = s.loadSessionState(activeSessionID)
   224→	require.NoError(t, err)
   225→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, activeState.Phase,
   226→		"ACTIVE session should transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED on GitCommit")
   227→
   228→	// Verify the IDLE session actually condensed (entire/sessions branch should exist)
   229→	idleState, err = s.loadSessionState(idleSessionID)
   230→	require.NoError(t, err)
   231→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   232→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after IDLE session condensation")
   233→	require.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   234→
   235→	// Verify IDLE session's StepCount was reset by condensation
   236→	assert.Equal(t, 0, idleState.StepCount,
   237→		"IDLE session StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   238→
   239→	// Verify shadow branch is NOT deleted because the ACTIVE session still needs it.
   240→	// After PostCommit, BaseCommit is updated to new HEAD via migration.
   241→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(activeState.BaseCommit, activeState.WorktreeID)
   242→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   243→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   244→	assert.NoError(t, err,
   245→		"shadow branch should be preserved when an active session still exists on it")
   246→}
   247→
   248→// TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that when
   249→// condensation fails (corrupted shadow branch), BaseCommit is NOT updated.
   250→func TestPostCommit_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   251→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   252→	t.Chdir(dir)
   253→
   254→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   255→	require.NoError(t, err)
   256→
   257→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   258→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-condense-fail-idle"
   259→
   260→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   261→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   262→
   263→	// Set phase to IDLE
   264→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   265→	require.NoError(t, err)
   266→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   267→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   268→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   269→
   270→	// Record original BaseCommit and StepCount before corruption
   271→	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
   272→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   273→
   274→	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
   275→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   276→	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
   277→	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
   278→
   279→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   280→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "e5f6a1b2c3d4")
   281→
   282→	// Run PostCommit
   283→	err = s.PostCommit()
   284→	require.NoError(t, err, "PostCommit should not return error even when condensation fails")
   285→
   286→	// Verify BaseCommit was NOT updated (condensation failed)
   287→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   288→	require.NoError(t, err)
   289→	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
   290→		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
   291→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   292→		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
   293→
   294→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (condensation failed)
   295→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   296→	require.Error(t, err,
   297→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
   298→
   299→	// Phase transition still applies even when condensation fails
   300→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
   301→		"phase should remain IDLE when condensation fails")
   302→}
   303→
   304→// TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit verifies that when
   305→// an IDLE session has no new transcript content since last condensation,
   306→// PostCommit skips condensation but still updates BaseCommit.
   307→func TestPostCommit_IdleSession_NoNewContent_UpdatesBaseCommit(t *testing.T) {
   308→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   309→	t.Chdir(dir)
   310→
   311→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   312→	require.NoError(t, err)
   313→
   314→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   315→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-idle-no-content"
   316→
   317→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   318→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   319→
   320→	// Set phase to IDLE with CheckpointTranscriptStart matching transcript length (2 lines)
   321→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   322→	require.NoError(t, err)
   323→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   324→	state.LastInteractionTime = nil
   325→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   326→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   327→
   328→	// Record shadow branch name
   329→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   330→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   331→
   332→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   333→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "f6a1b2c3d4e5")
   334→
   335→	// Get new HEAD hash for comparison
   336→	head, err := repo.Head()
   337→	require.NoError(t, err)
   338→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   339→
   340→	// Run PostCommit
   341→	err = s.PostCommit()
   342→	require.NoError(t, err)
   343→
   344→	// Verify BaseCommit was updated to new HEAD
   345→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   346→	require.NoError(t, err)
   347→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   348→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD when no new content")
   349→
   350→	// Shadow branch should still exist (not deleted, no condensation)
   351→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   352→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   353→	require.NoError(t, err,
   354→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   355→
   356→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   357→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   358→	require.Error(t, err,
   359→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no condensation happened")
   360→
   361→	// StepCount should be unchanged
   362→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   363→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   364→}

   366→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses verifies that an ENDED
   367→// session with files touched and new content condenses on commit.
   368→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_Condenses(t *testing.T) {
   369→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   370→	t.Chdir(dir)
   371→
   372→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   373→	require.NoError(t, err)
   374→
   375→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   376→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ended-condenses"
   377→
   378→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   379→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   380→
   381→	// Set phase to ENDED with files touched
   382→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   383→	require.NoError(t, err)
   384→	now := time.Now()
   385→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   386→	state.EndedAt = &now
   387→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   388→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   389→
   390→	// Record shadow branch name
   391→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   392→
   393→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   394→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f7")
   395→
   396→	// Run PostCommit
   397→	err = s.PostCommit()
   398→	require.NoError(t, err)
   399→
   400→	// Verify entire/sessions branch exists (condensation happened)
   401→	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   402→	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after condensation")
   403→	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
   404→
   405→	// Verify old shadow branch is deleted after condensation
   406→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   407→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   408→	require.Error(t, err,
   409→		"shadow branch should be deleted after condensation for ENDED session")
   410→
   411→	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
   412→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   413→	require.NoError(t, err)
   414→	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
   415→		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
   416→
   417→	// Phase stays ENDED
   418→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseEnded, state.Phase,
   419→		"ENDED session should stay ENDED after condensation")
   420→}

   422→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent verifies that an ENDED
   423→// session with files touched but no new transcript content skips condensation
   424→// and updates BaseCommit via fallthrough.
   425→func TestPostCommit_EndedSession_FilesTouched_NoNewContent(t *testing.T) {
   426→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)

   428→
   429→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)

   441→	now := time.Now()
   442→	state.Phase = session.PhaseEnded
   443→	state.EndedAt = &now
   444→	state.FilesTouched = []string{"test.txt"}
   445→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = 2 // Transcript has exactly 2 lines
   446→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   447→
   448→	// Record shadow branch name and original StepCount
   449→	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   450→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   451→
   452→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   453→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a2")
   454→
   455→	// Get new HEAD hash
   456→	head, err := repo.Head()
   457→	require.NoError(t, err)
   458→	newHeadHash := head.Hash().String()
   459→
   460→	// Run PostCommit
   461→	err = s.PostCommit()
   462→	require.NoError(t, err)
   463→
   464→	// Verify entire/sessions branch does NOT exist (no condensation)
   465→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   466→	require.Error(t, err,
   467→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when no new content")
   468→
   469→	// Shadow branch should still exist
   470→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
   471→	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
   472→	require.NoError(t, err,
   473→		"shadow branch should still exist when no condensation happened")
   474→
   475→	// BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough
   476→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   477→	require.NoError(t, err)
   478→	assert.Equal(t, newHeadHash, state.BaseCommit,
   479→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD via fallthrough")
   480→
   481→	// StepCount unchanged
   482→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   483→		"StepCount should be unchanged when no condensation happened")
   484→}

   486→// TestPostCommit_EndedSession_NoFilesTouched_Discards verifies that an ENDED

   545→// TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch verifies that an
   546→// ACTIVE_COMMITTED session receiving another commit migrates the shadow branch
   547→// to the new HEAD and stays in ACTIVE_COMMITTED.
   548→func TestPostCommit_ActiveCommitted_MigratesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
   549→	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
   550→	t.Chdir(dir)
   551→
   552→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
   553→	require.NoError(t, err)
   554→
   555→	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
   556→	sessionID := "test-postcommit-ac-migrate"
   557→
   558→	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
   559→	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
   560→
   561→	// Set phase to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   562→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   563→	require.NoError(t, err)
   564→	state.Phase = session.PhaseActiveCommitted
   565→	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
   566→
   567→	// Record original shadow branch name and BaseCommit
   568→	originalShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   569→	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
   570→
   571→	// Create a commit with the checkpoint trailer
   572→	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "d4e5f6a1b2c4")
   573→
   574→	// Run PostCommit
   575→	err = s.PostCommit()
   576→	require.NoError(t, err)
   577→
   578→	// Verify phase stays ACTIVE_COMMITTED
   579→	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   580→	require.NoError(t, err)
   581→	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase,
   582→		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED session should stay ACTIVE_COMMITTED on subsequent commit")
   583→
   584→	// Verify BaseCommit updated to new HEAD
   585→	head, err := repo.Head()
   586→	require.NoError(t, err)
   587→	assert.Equal(t, head.Hash().String(), state.BaseCommit,
   588→		"BaseCommit should be updated to new HEAD after migration")
   589→
   590→	// Verify new shadow branch exists at new HEAD hash
   591→	newShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   592→	newRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)
   593→	_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)
   594→	require.NoError(t, err,
   595→		"new shadow branch should exist after migration")
   596→
   597→	// Verify original shadow branch no longer exists (was migrated/renamed)
   598→	oldRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(originalShadowBranch)
   599→	_, err = repo.Reference(oldRefName, true)
   600→	require.Error(t, err,
   601→		"original shadow branch should no longer exist after migration")
   602→
   603→	// StepCount unchanged (no condensation)
   604→	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
   605→		"StepCount should be unchanged - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   606→
   607→	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (no condensation)
   608→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   609→	require.Error(t, err,
   610→		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist - no condensation for ACTIVE_COMMITTED")
   611→}

   663→	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
   664→	require.Error(t, err,

   672→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
   673→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
   674→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
   675→	t.Helper()
   676→
   677→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
   678→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   679→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   680→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   681→
   682→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
   683→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}
   684→`
   685→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
   686→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   687→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
   688→
   689→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
   690→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   691→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   692→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   693→		NewFiles:       []string{},
   694→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   695→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   696→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   697→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
   698→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   699→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   700→	})
   701→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
   702→}
   703→
   704→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
   705→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
   706→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
   707→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {
   708→	t.Helper()
   709→
   710→	cpID := id.MustCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   711→
   712→	// Modify a file so there is something to commit
   713→	testFile := filepath.Join(dir, "test.txt")
   714→	content := "updated at " + time.Now().String()
   715→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(testFile, []byte(content), 0o644))
   716→
   717→	wt, err := repo.Worktree()
   718→	require.NoError(t, err)
   719→
   720→	_, err = wt.Add("test.txt")
   721→	require.NoError(t, err)
   722→
   723→	commitMsg := "test commit\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + cpID.String() + "\n"
   724→	_, err = wt.Commit(commitMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   725→		Author: &object.Signature{

   727→			Email: "test@test.com",
   728→			When:  time.Now(),
   729→		},
   730→	})
   731→	require.NoError(t, err, "commit with checkpoint trailer should succeed")
   732→}
```

```
   219→func transitionFromActiveCommitted(event Event, ctx TransitionContext) TransitionResult {
   220→	switch event {
   221→	case EventTurnStart:
   222→		// Ctrl-C recovery after commit.
   223→		return TransitionResult{
   224→			NewPhase: PhaseActive,
   225→			Actions:  []Action{ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   226→		}
   227→	case EventTurnEnd:
   228→		return TransitionResult{
   229→			NewPhase: PhaseIdle,
   230→			Actions:  []Action{ActionCondense, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   231→		}
   232→	case EventGitCommit:
   233→		if ctx.IsRebaseInProgress {
   234→			return TransitionResult{NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted}
   235→		}
   236→		return TransitionResult{
   237→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   238→			Actions:  []Action{ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionUpdateLastInteraction},
   239→		}
   240→	case EventSessionStart:
   241→		return TransitionResult{
   242→			NewPhase: PhaseActiveCommitted,
   243→			Actions:  []Action{ActionWarnStaleSession},
   244→		}
```

```
   290→}
   291→
   292→// ApplyCommonActions applies the common (non-strategy-specific) actions from a
   293→// TransitionResult to the given State. It updates Phase, LastInteractionTime,
   294→// and EndedAt as indicated by the transition.
   295→//
   296→// Returns the subset of actions that require strategy-specific handling
   297→// (e.g., ActionCondense, ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession).
   298→// The caller is responsible for dispatching those.
   299→func ApplyCommonActions(state *State, result TransitionResult) []Action {
   300→	state.Phase = result.NewPhase
   301→
   302→	var remaining []Action
   303→	for _, action := range result.Actions {
   304→		switch action {
   305→		case ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
   306→			now := time.Now()
   307→			state.LastInteractionTime = &now
   308→		case ActionClearEndedAt:
   309→			state.EndedAt = nil
   310→		case ActionCondense, ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, ActionDiscardIfNoFiles,
   311→			ActionMigrateShadowBranch, ActionWarnStaleSession:
   312→			// Strategy-specific actions — pass through to caller.
   313→			remaining = append(remaining, action)
   314→		}
   315→	}
   316→	return remaining
   317→}
```

```
     1→package strategy
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→
     9→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    10→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/session"
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/validation"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// Session state management functions shared across all strategies.
    15→// SessionState is stored in .git/entire-sessions/{session_id}.json
    16→
    17→// getSessionStateDir returns the path to the session state directory.
    18→// This is stored in the git common dir so it's shared across all worktrees.
    19→func getSessionStateDir() (string, error) {
    20→	commonDir, err := GetGitCommonDir()
    21→	if err != nil {
    22→		return "", err
    23→	}
    24→	return filepath.Join(commonDir, session.SessionStateDirName), nil
    25→}
    26→
    27→// sessionStateFile returns the path to a session state file.
    28→func sessionStateFile(sessionID string) (string, error) {
    29→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		return "", err
    32→	}
    33→	return filepath.Join(stateDir, sessionID+".json"), nil
    34→}
    35→
    36→// LoadSessionState loads the session state for the given session ID.
    37→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
    38→func LoadSessionState(sessionID string) (*SessionState, error) {
    39→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    40→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(sessionID)
    45→	if err != nil {
    46→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
    47→	}
    48→
    49→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID, not user input
    50→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
    51→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
    52→	}
    53→	if err != nil {
    54→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	var state SessionState
    58→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
    59→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
    62→	return &state, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// SaveSessionState saves the session state atomically.
    66→func SaveSessionState(state *SessionState) error {
    67→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
    68→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
    69→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	stateDir, err := getSessionStateDir()
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state directory: %w", err)
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if err := os.MkdirAll(stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
    78→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
    82→	if err != nil {
    83→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(state.SessionID)
    87→	if err != nil {
    88→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
    92→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
    93→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
    94→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
    97→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
    98→	}
    99→	return nil
   100→}
   101→
   102→// ClearSessionState removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   103→func ClearSessionState(sessionID string) error {
   104→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   105→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   106→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	stateFile, err := sessionStateFile(sessionID)
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session state file path: %w", err)
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   115→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   116→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   117→		}
   118→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   119→	}
   120→	return nil
   121→}
```

```
    24→// PhaseFromString normalizes a phase string, treating empty or unknown values
    25→// as PhaseIdle for backward compatibility with pre-state-machine session files.
    26→func PhaseFromString(s string) Phase {
    27→	switch Phase(s) {
    28→	case PhaseActive:
    29→		return PhaseActive
    30→	case PhaseActiveCommitted:
    31→		return PhaseActiveCommitted
    32→	case PhaseIdle:
    33→		return PhaseIdle
    34→	case PhaseEnded:
    35→		return PhaseEnded
    36→	default:
    37→		return PhaseIdle
    38→	}
    39→}
    40→
    41→// IsActive reports whether the phase represents an active agent turn.
    42→func (p Phase) IsActive() bool {
    43→	return p == PhaseActive || p == PhaseActiveCommitted
    44→}

    46→// Event represents something that happened to a session.
```

```
   463→// LogsOnlyRestorer is an optional interface for strategies that support
   464→// restoring session logs without file state restoration.
   465→// This is used for "logs-only" rewind points where only the session transcript
   466→// can be restored (file state requires git checkout).
   467→type LogsOnlyRestorer interface {
   468→	// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   469→	// Does not modify the working directory - only restores the transcript
   470→	// to Claude's project directory.
   471→	// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   472→	RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error
   473→}

   475→// SessionResetter is an optional interface for strategies that support
   476→// resetting session state and shadow branches.
   477→// This is used by the "rewind reset" command to clean up shadow branches
   478→// and session state when a user wants to start fresh.
   479→type SessionResetter interface {
   480→	// Reset deletes the shadow branch and session state for the current HEAD.
   481→	// Returns nil if there's nothing to reset (no shadow branch).
   482→	Reset() error
   483→}

   485→// ConcurrentSessionChecker is an optional interface for strategies that support
   486→// counting concurrent sessions with uncommitted changes.
   487→// This is used by the SessionStart hook to show an informational message about
   488→// how many other active conversations will be included in the next commit.
   489→type ConcurrentSessionChecker interface {
   490→	// CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints returns the number of other active sessions
   491→	// with uncommitted checkpoints on the same base commit.
   492→	// Returns 0, nil if no such sessions exist.
   493→	CountOtherActiveSessionsWithCheckpoints(currentSessionID string) (int, error)
   494→}
   495→
   496→// SessionSource is an optional interface for strategies that provide additional
   497→// sessions beyond those stored on the entire/sessions branch.
   498→// For example, manual-commit strategy provides active sessions from .git/entire-sessions/
   499→// that haven't yet been condensed to entire/sessions.
   500→//
   501→// ListSessions() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks if they
   502→// implement SessionSource, and merges their additional sessions by ID.
   503→type SessionSource interface {
   504→	// GetAdditionalSessions returns sessions not yet on entire/sessions branch.
   505→	GetAdditionalSessions() ([]*Session, error)
   506→}

   508→// OrphanedItemsLister is an optional interface for strategies that can identify

   511→//
   512→// ListAllCleanupItems() automatically discovers all registered strategies, checks
```

Now I need to understand the `condenseAndUpdateState` helper and how PostCommit calls it to closely replicate the pattern. The key things are:

1. Open repo, get HEAD
2. Generate/use checkpoint ID from `state.PendingCheckpointID`
3. Check `sessionHasNewContent`
4. Call `condenseAndUpdateState` which calls `CondenseSession` and updates state fields
5. Delete shadow branches

Let me add `HandleTurnEnd` at the end of the file (before the final `hasOverlappingFiles`):

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01EfkB51MvKh4pktyVy7uw2G
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go",
  "old_string": "// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.\nfunc hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {",
  "new_string": "// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.\n// This handles the ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE transition where ActionCondense is deferred\n// from PostCommit (agent was still active during the commit).\n//\n//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState, actions []session.Action) error {\n\tif len(actions) == 0 {\n\t\treturn nil\n\t}\n\n\tlogCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), \"checkpoint\")\n\n\tfor _, action := range actions {\n\t\tswitch action {\n\t\tcase session.ActionCondense:\n\t\t\ts.handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx, state)\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"turn-end: unhandled action\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"action\", action.String()),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleTurnEndCondense performs deferred condensation at turn end.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState) {\n\trepo, err := OpenRepository()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"turn-end condense: failed to open repo\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\thead, err := repo.Head()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"turn-end condense: failed to get HEAD\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", err.Error()))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Derive checkpoint ID from PendingCheckpointID (set during PostCommit),\n\t// or generate a new one if not set.\n\tvar checkpointID id.CheckpointID\n\tif state.PendingCheckpointID != \"\" {\n\t\tif cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); parseErr == nil {\n\t\t\tcheckpointID = cpID\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif checkpointID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\tcpID, genErr := id.Generate()\n\t\tif genErr != nil {\n\t\t\tlogging.Warn(logCtx, \"turn-end condense: failed to generate checkpoint ID\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", genErr.Error()))\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tcheckpointID = cpID\n\t}\n\n\t// Check if there is actually new content to condense.\n\t// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content so we don't silently skip.\n\thasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)\n\tif contentErr != nil {\n\t\thasNew = true\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"turn-end condense: error checking content, assuming new\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID),\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"error\", contentErr.Error()))\n\t}\n\n\tif !hasNew {\n\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"turn-end condense: no new content\",\n\t\t\tslog.String(\"session_id\", state.SessionID))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tshadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)\n\tshadowBranchesToDelete := map[string]struct{}{}\n\n\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)\n\n\t// Delete shadow branches after condensation\n\tfor branchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {\n\t\tif err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, branchName); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\\n\", branchName, err)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"shadow branch deleted (turn-end)\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", \"manual-commit\"),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"shadow_branch\", branchName),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.\nfunc hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {\n\tturnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)\n\tif loadErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\\n\", loadErr)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif turnState == nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tresult := session.Transition(turnState.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(turnState, result)\n\n\t// Dispatch strategy-specific actions (e.g., ActionCondense for ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE)\n\tif len(remaining) > 0 {\n\t\tstrat := GetStrategy()\n\t\tif handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {\n\t\t\tif err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState, remaining); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\\n\", updateErr)\n\t}\n}"
```

```
   672→// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint
   673→// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
   674→func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {
   675→	t.Helper()
   676→
   677→	// Create metadata directory with a transcript file
   678→	metadataDir := ".entire/metadata/" + sessionID
   679→	metadataDirAbs := filepath.Join(dir, metadataDir)
   680→	require.NoError(t, os.MkdirAll(metadataDirAbs, 0o755))
   681→
   682→	transcript := `{"type":"human","message":{"content":"test prompt"}}
   683→{"type":"assistant","message":{"content":"test response"}}

   685→	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(
   686→		filepath.Join(metadataDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName),
   687→		[]byte(transcript), 0o644))
   688→
   689→	// SaveChanges creates the shadow branch and checkpoint
   690→	err := s.SaveChanges(SaveContext{
   691→		SessionID:      sessionID,
   692→		ModifiedFiles:  []string{},
   693→		NewFiles:       []string{},
   694→		DeletedFiles:   []string{},
   695→		MetadataDir:    metadataDir,
   696→		MetadataDirAbs: metadataDirAbs,
   697→		CommitMessage:  "Checkpoint 1",
   698→		AuthorName:     "Test",
   699→		AuthorEmail:    "test@test.com",
   700→	})
   701→	require.NoError(t, err, "SaveChanges should succeed to create shadow branch content")
   702→}
   703→
   704→// commitWithCheckpointTrailer creates a commit on the current branch with the
   705→// Entire-Checkpoint trailer in the commit message. This simulates what happens
   706→// after PrepareCommitMsg adds the trailer and the user completes the commit.
   707→func commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t *testing.T, repo *git.Repository, dir, checkpointIDStr string) {

   728→			When:  time.Now(),
   729→		},
   730→	})
```

```json
  "new_string": "// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd\n// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session\n// to entire/sessions and cleans up the shadow branch.\nfunc TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-turnend-condenses\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Simulate PostCommit: create a commit with trailer and transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit so phase transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED and PendingCheckpointID is set\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)\n\n\t// Now simulate the TurnEnd transition that the handler dispatches\n\tresult := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)\n\n\t// Verify the state machine emits ActionCondense\n\trequire.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense,\n\t\t\"ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd should emit ActionCondense\")\n\n\t// Record shadow branch name BEFORE HandleTurnEnd (BaseCommit may change)\n\tshadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)\n\n\t// Call HandleTurnEnd with the remaining actions\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify condensation happened: entire/sessions branch should exist\n\tsessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"entire/sessions branch should exist after turn-end condensation\")\n\tassert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)\n\n\t// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tassert.Error(t, err,\n\t\t\"shadow branch should be deleted after turn-end condensation\")\n\n\t// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be reset after condensation\")\n\n\t// Verify phase is IDLE (set by ApplyCommonActions above)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,\n\t\t\"phase should be IDLE after TurnEnd\")\n}\n\n// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies\n// that when HandleTurnEnd condensation fails, BaseCommit is NOT updated and\n// the shadow branch is preserved.\nfunc TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-turnend-condense-fail\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"b2c3d4e5f6a1\")\n\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)\n\n\t// Record original BaseCommit before corruption\n\toriginalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit\n\toriginalStepCount := state.StepCount\n\n\t// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash\n\tshadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)\n\tcorruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)\n\trequire.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))\n\n\t// Run the transition\n\tresult := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)\n\n\t// Call HandleTurnEnd — condensation should fail silently\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err, \"HandleTurnEnd should not return error even when condensation fails\")\n\n\t// BaseCommit should NOT be updated (condensation failed)\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails\")\n\n\t// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (condensation failed)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)\n\tassert.Error(t, err,\n\t\t\"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails\")\n}\n\n// TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions verifies that HandleTurnEnd with no actions\n// is a no-op (normal ACTIVE → IDLE transition has no strategy-specific actions).\nfunc TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-turnend-no-actions\"\n\n\t// Initialize session and save a checkpoint\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE (normal turn, no commit during turn)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\toriginalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit\n\toriginalStepCount := state.StepCount\n\n\t// ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE with no strategy-specific actions\n\tresult := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)\n\n\t// Verify no strategy-specific actions for ACTIVE → IDLE\n\tassert.Empty(t, remaining,\n\t\t\"ACTIVE + TurnEnd should not emit strategy-specific actions\")\n\n\t// Call HandleTurnEnd with empty actions — should be a no-op\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify state is unchanged\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,\n\t\t\"BaseCommit should be unchanged for no-op turn end\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be unchanged for no-op turn end\")\n\n\t// Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up)\n\tshadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tassert.NoError(t, err,\n\t\t\"shadow branch should still exist after no-op turn end\")\n}\n\n// setupSessionWithCheckpoint initializes a session and creates one checkpoint\n// on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation."
}
```

> AGENT

All done. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Summary

**Bug**: `transitionSessionTurnEnd()` in `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go:751` was ignoring the return value of `ApplyCommonActions()`. When a session in `ACTIVE_COMMITTED` phase received `EventTurnEnd`, the state machine correctly emitted `ActionCondense`, but the action was never dispatched. Session data from in-turn commits stayed stuck on the shadow branch.

**Fix** (4 files):

1. **`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/strategy.go`** — Added `TurnEndHandler` optional interface with `HandleTurnEnd(state *session.State, actions []session.Action) error`, following the established pattern for `PostCommitHandler`, `PrePushHandler`, etc.

2. **`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go`** — Implemented `HandleTurnEnd` on `ManualCommitStrategy`. Dispatches `ActionCondense` by opening the repo, deriving/generating a checkpoint ID from `PendingCheckpointID`, checking for new content, running `condenseAndUpdateState`, and cleaning up shadow branches.

3. **`cmd/entire/cli/hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`** — Updated `transitionSessionTurnEnd()` to capture the `remaining` actions from `ApplyCommonActions()` and dispatch them via `TurnEndHandler` type assertion on the current strategy.

4. **`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go`** — Added 3 tests:
   - `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession` — verifies full condensation flow
   - `TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch` — verifies BaseCommit preserved on failure
   - `TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions` — verifies no-op for normal ACTIVE → IDLE

> DEVELOPER

commit, push.

How did we miss this btw?

> AGENT

Good question. The bug slipped through because of how the code evolved:

1. **`ApplyCommonActions` was originally void-like in usage.** When the state machine was first wired up, `transitionSessionTurnEnd` was written to just fire-and-forget the transition — `ACTIVE → IDLE` has no strategy-specific actions, so ignoring the return was harmless at that point.

2. **`ACTIVE_COMMITTED` was added later** (as part of the PostCommit phase-aware hooks work). That transition introduced `ActionCondense` as a deferred action, but `transitionSessionTurnEnd` wasn't updated to handle the new return value. The PostCommit path got the full action dispatch loop, but the TurnEnd path was overlooked.

3. **No test covered the end-to-end path.** The state machine unit tests verified that `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd` emits `ActionCondense` (the transition table was correct). The PostCommit tests verified condensation works. But nothing tested that `transitionSessionTurnEnd` actually *dispatches* the actions it receives — the integration gap between "state machine says condense" and "condensation actually fires."

4. **Silent failure.** The condensation just... didn't happen. No error, no warning. The session data sat on the shadow branch until the next manual commit, which would condense it anyway. So the bug was masked in the common case — you'd only notice if you looked at `entire/sessions` timing or if a session ended without a subsequent commit.

Essentially: correct state machine + correct condensation logic + missing glue code = silent data loss. The kind of bug that compile-time interface checks (ENT-250) would catch.

Let me commit and push.

```
 	return sessionData.Prompts[len(sessionData.Prompts)-1]

+// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted when an agent turn ends.
+// This handles the ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE transition where ActionCondense is deferred
+// from PostCommit (agent was still active during the commit).
+//
+//nolint:unparam // error return required by interface but hooks must return nil
+func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) HandleTurnEnd(state *SessionState, actions []session.Action) error {
+	if len(actions) == 0 {
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
+
+	for _, action := range actions {
+		switch action {
+		case session.ActionCondense:
+			s.handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx, state)
+		case session.ActionCondenseIfFilesTouched, session.ActionDiscardIfNoFiles,
+			session.ActionMigrateShadowBranch, session.ActionWarnStaleSession:
+			// Not expected at turn-end; log for diagnostics.
+			logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end: unexpected action",
+				slog.String("action", action.String()),
+				slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
+			)
+		case session.ActionClearEndedAt, session.ActionUpdateLastInteraction:
+			// Handled by session.ApplyCommonActions before this is called.
+		}
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
+// handleTurnEndCondense performs deferred condensation at turn end.
+func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) handleTurnEndCondense(logCtx context.Context, state *SessionState) {
+	repo, err := OpenRepository()
+	if err != nil {
+		logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to open repo",
+			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
+		return
+	}
+
+	head, err := repo.Head()
+	if err != nil {
+		logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to get HEAD",
+			slog.String("error", err.Error()))
+		return
+	}
+
+	// Derive checkpoint ID from PendingCheckpointID (set during PostCommit),
+	// or generate a new one if not set.
+	var checkpointID id.CheckpointID
+	if state.PendingCheckpointID != "" {
+		if cpID, parseErr := id.NewCheckpointID(state.PendingCheckpointID); parseErr == nil {
+			checkpointID = cpID
+		}
+	}
+	if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
+		cpID, genErr := id.Generate()
+		if genErr != nil {
+			logging.Warn(logCtx, "turn-end condense: failed to generate checkpoint ID",
+				slog.String("error", genErr.Error()))
+			return
+		}
+		checkpointID = cpID
+	}
+
+	// Check if there is actually new content to condense.
+	// Fail-open: if content check errors, assume new content so we don't silently skip.
+	hasNew, contentErr := s.sessionHasNewContent(repo, state)
+	if contentErr != nil {
+		hasNew = true
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end condense: error checking content, assuming new",
+			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
+			slog.String("error", contentErr.Error()))
+	}
+
+	if !hasNew {
+		logging.Debug(logCtx, "turn-end condense: no new content",
+			slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
+		return
+	}
+
+	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	shadowBranchesToDelete := map[string]struct{}{}
+
+	s.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)
+
+	// Delete shadow branches after condensation
+	for branchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {
+		if err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, branchName); err != nil {
+			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\n", branchName, err)
+		} else {
+			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\n", branchName)
+			logging.Info(logCtx, "shadow branch deleted (turn-end)",
+				slog.String("strategy", "manual-commit"),
+				slog.String("shadow_branch", branchName),
+			)
+		}
+	}
+}
+
 // hasOverlappingFiles checks if any file in stagedFiles appears in filesTouched.
 func hasOverlappingFiles(stagedFiles, filesTouched []string) bool {
 	touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)

+// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd
+// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session
+// to entire/sessions and cleans up the shadow branch.
+func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-turnend-condenses"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint so there is shadow branch content
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Simulate PostCommit: create a commit with trailer and transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "a1b2c3d4e5f6")
+
+	// Run PostCommit so phase transitions to ACTIVE_COMMITTED and PendingCheckpointID is set
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
+
+	// Now simulate the TurnEnd transition that the handler dispatches
+	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
+	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
+
+	// Verify the state machine emits ActionCondense
+	require.Contains(t, remaining, session.ActionCondense,
+		"ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd should emit ActionCondense")
+
+	// Record shadow branch name BEFORE HandleTurnEnd (BaseCommit may change)
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+
+	// Call HandleTurnEnd with the remaining actions
+	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify condensation happened: entire/sessions branch should exist
+	sessionsRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	require.NoError(t, err, "entire/sessions branch should exist after turn-end condensation")
+	assert.NotNil(t, sessionsRef)
+
+	// Verify shadow branch IS deleted after condensation
+	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
+	require.Error(t, err,
+		"shadow branch should be deleted after turn-end condensation")
+
+	// Verify StepCount was reset by condensation
+	assert.Equal(t, 0, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be reset after condensation")
+
+	// Verify phase is IDLE (set by ApplyCommonActions above)
+	assert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state.Phase,
+		"phase should be IDLE after TurnEnd")
+}
+
+// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch verifies
+// that when HandleTurnEnd condensation fails, BaseCommit is NOT updated and
+// the shadow branch is preserved.
+func TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensationFailure_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-turnend-condense-fail"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	commitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, "b2c3d4e5f6a1")
+
+	err = s.PostCommit()
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	state, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	require.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)
+
+	// Record original BaseCommit before corruption
+	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// Corrupt shadow branch by pointing it at ZeroHash
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	corruptRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch), plumbing.ZeroHash)
+	require.NoError(t, repo.Storer.SetReference(corruptRef))
+
+	// Run the transition
+	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
+	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
+
+	// Call HandleTurnEnd — condensation should fail silently
+	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
+	require.NoError(t, err, "HandleTurnEnd should not return error even when condensation fails")
+
+	// BaseCommit should NOT be updated (condensation failed)
+	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should NOT be updated when condensation fails")
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should NOT be reset when condensation fails")
+
+	// entire/sessions branch should NOT exist (condensation failed)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName), true)
+	assert.Error(t, err,
+		"entire/sessions branch should NOT exist when condensation fails")
+}
+
+// TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions verifies that HandleTurnEnd with no actions
+// is a no-op (normal ACTIVE → IDLE transition has no strategy-specific actions).
+func TestTurnEnd_Active_NoActions(t *testing.T) {
+	dir := setupGitRepo(t)
+	t.Chdir(dir)
+
+	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	s := &ManualCommitStrategy{}
+	sessionID := "test-turnend-no-actions"
+
+	// Initialize session and save a checkpoint
+	setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)
+
+	// Set phase to ACTIVE (normal turn, no commit during turn)
+	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+	state.Phase = session.PhaseActive
+	require.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))
+
+	originalBaseCommit := state.BaseCommit
+	originalStepCount := state.StepCount
+
+	// ACTIVE + TurnEnd → IDLE with no strategy-specific actions
+	result := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
+	remaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)
+
+	// Verify no strategy-specific actions for ACTIVE → IDLE
+	assert.Empty(t, remaining,
+		"ACTIVE + TurnEnd should not emit strategy-specific actions")
+
+	// Call HandleTurnEnd with empty actions — should be a no-op
+	err = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)
+	require.NoError(t, err)
+
+	// Verify state is unchanged
+	assert.Equal(t, originalBaseCommit, state.BaseCommit,
+		"BaseCommit should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
+	assert.Equal(t, originalStepCount, state.StepCount,
+		"StepCount should be unchanged for no-op turn end")
+
+	// Shadow branch should still exist (not cleaned up)
+	shadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(originalBaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
+	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)
+	_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)
+	assert.NoError(t, err,
+		"shadow branch should still exist after no-op turn end")
+}
+

 // on the shadow branch so there is content available for condensation.
 func setupSessionWithCheckpoint(t *testing.T, s *ManualCommitStrategy, _ *git.Repository, dir, sessionID string) {

+// TurnEndHandler is an optional interface for strategies that need to
+// handle deferred actions when an agent turn ends.
+// For example, manual-commit strategy uses this to condense session data
+// that was deferred during ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE transitions.
+type TurnEndHandler interface {
+	// HandleTurnEnd dispatches strategy-specific actions emitted by the
+	// ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE (or other) turn-end transition.
+	// The state has already been updated by ApplyCommonActions; the caller
+	// saves it after this method returns.
+	HandleTurnEnd(state *session.State, actions []session.Action) error
+}
+
```

> AGENT

Yeah. Line 230: `TurnEnd_transitions_to_IDLE_with_condense` — the state machine test was there from day one, asserting that `ACTIVE_COMMITTED + TurnEnd` emits `ActionCondense`. We designed and tested the requirement at the state machine level. The gap was one layer up: nobody tested that `transitionSessionTurnEnd()` actually does something with what the state machine returns.

So the answer to "how did we miss this" is more precise than what I said earlier: **we didn't miss the requirement — we had the test. But the test was at the wrong layer.** The state machine test proved the transition table was correct. What was missing was a wiring test that verified the caller dispatches the actions. The state machine was essentially screaming "please condense!" and the caller was nodding and throwing the note in the trash.

The `handleAmendCommitMsg` function treats all `source=\"commit\"` cases as amend, so `git commit -C <sha>` (verbatim message reuse, no editor) could silently inject a trailer from `PendingCheckpointID` into a message the user intended to reuse as-is.   "id": 2779182556,

```
  "body": "### PostCommit omits PendingCheckpointID for deferred condensation path\n\n**High Severity**\n\n<!-- DESCRIPTION START -->\nWhen `PostCommit` handles `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` (ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED), it never stores the commit's `checkpointID` into `state.PendingCheckpointID`. Later, `handleTurnEndCondense` tries to derive the checkpoint ID from `PendingCheckpointID` — finding it empty, it generates a brand-new ID. This creates a mismatch: the commit trailer says `Entire-Checkpoint: X`, but the condensed data is stored under a different ID `Y`. Any lookup by the commit's trailer ID will find nothing.\n<!-- DESCRIPTION END -->\n\n<!-- BUGBOT_BUG_ID: d853260b-09ae-4a0e-957b-fb1ee5774ad5 -->\n\n<!-- LOCATIONS START\ncmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go#L612-L616\ncmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go#L1256-L1273\nLOCATIONS END -->\n<details>\n<summary>Additional Locations (1)</summary>\n\n- [`cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go#L1256-L1273`](https://github.REDACTED.go#L1256-L1273)\n\n</details>\n\n<p><a href=\"https://cursor.com/open?REDACTED.REDACTED.REDACTED\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Cursor\" width=\"115\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-cursor-dark.png\"></picture></a>&nbsp;<a href=\"https://cursor.com/agents?REDACTED.REDACTED.REDACTED\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"><picture><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: dark)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"><source media=\"(prefers-color-scheme: light)\" srcset=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-light.png\"><img alt=\"Fix in Web\" width=\"99\" height=\"28\" src=\"https://cursor.com/assets/images/fix-in-web-dark.png\"></picture></a></p>\n\n"
```

> AGENT

The bot is right. When `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` fires (ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED), we defer the migration but never store `checkpointID` into `state.PendingCheckpointID`. Later, `handleTurnEndCondense` falls through to generating a new ID. The commit trailer and the condensed metadata would point at different IDs.

3. Write tests for both

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_hooks.go",
  "old_string": "\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)\n\n\t// Delete shadow branches after condensation\n\tfor branchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {\n\t\tif err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, branchName); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\\n\", branchName, err)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"shadow branch deleted (turn-end)\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", \"manual-commit\"),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"shadow_branch\", branchName),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "\ts.condenseAndUpdateState(logCtx, repo, checkpointID, state, head, shadowBranchName, shadowBranchesToDelete)\n\n\t// Delete shadow branches after condensation — but only if no other active\n\t// sessions share the branch (same safety check PostCommit uses).\n\tfor branchName := range shadowBranchesToDelete {\n\t\tif s.hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch(state.SessionID, state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID) {\n\t\t\tlogging.Debug(logCtx, \"turn-end: preserving shadow branch (other active session exists)\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"shadow_branch\", branchName))\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := deleteShadowBranch(repo, branchName); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Warning: failed to clean up %s: %v\\n\", branchName, err)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[entire] Cleaned up shadow branch: %s\\n\", branchName)\n\t\t\tlogging.Info(logCtx, \"shadow branch deleted (turn-end)\",\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"strategy\", \"manual-commit\"),\n\t\t\t\tslog.String(\"shadow_branch\", branchName),\n\t\t\t)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch checks if any other sessions with the same\n// base commit and worktree ID are in an active phase. Used to prevent deleting\n// a shadow branch that another session still needs.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch(currentSessionID, baseCommit, worktreeID string) bool {\n\tsessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(baseCommit)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn false // Fail-open: if we can't check, don't block deletion\n\t}\n\tfor _, other := range sessions {\n\t\tif other.SessionID == currentSessionID {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif other.WorktreeID == worktreeID && other.Phase.IsActive() {\n\t\t\treturn true\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn false\n}"
}
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/alex/workspace/cli/.worktrees/3/cmd/entire/cli/strategy/phase_postcommit_test.go",
  "old_string": "// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd\n// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session\n// to entire/sessions and cleans up the shadow branch.",
  "new_string": "// TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID verifies that PostCommit\n// stores PendingCheckpointID when transitioning ACTIVE → ACTIVE_COMMITTED.\n// This ensures HandleTurnEnd can reuse the same checkpoint ID that's in the\n// commit trailer, rather than generating a mismatched one.\nfunc TestPostCommit_ActiveSession_SetsPendingCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-postcommit-pending-cpid\"\n\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Set phase to ACTIVE (agent mid-turn)\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\tstate.PendingCheckpointID = \"\" // Ensure it starts empty\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\t// Create a commit with a known checkpoint ID\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\")\n\n\t// Run PostCommit\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify phase transitioned to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state.Phase)\n\n\t// Verify PendingCheckpointID was stored from the commit trailer\n\tassert.Equal(t, \"a1b2c3d4e5f6\", state.PendingCheckpointID,\n\t\t\"PendingCheckpointID should be set to the commit's checkpoint ID for deferred condensation\")\n}\n\n// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID verifies that HandleTurnEnd\n// uses PendingCheckpointID (set by PostCommit) rather than generating a new one.\n// This ensures the condensed metadata matches the commit trailer.\nfunc TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_ReusesCheckpointID(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID := \"test-turnend-reuses-cpid\"\n\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID)\n\n\t// Simulate PostCommit: transition to ACTIVE_COMMITTED with PendingCheckpointID\n\tstate, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tstate.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state))\n\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"d4e5f6a1b2c3\")\n\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tstate, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, \"d4e5f6a1b2c3\", state.PendingCheckpointID)\n\n\t// Run TurnEnd\n\tresult := session.Transition(state.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state, result)\n\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify the condensed checkpoint ID matches the commit trailer\n\t// The LastCheckpointID is set by condenseAndUpdateState on success\n\tassert.Equal(t, id.CheckpointID(\"d4e5f6a1b2c3\"), state.LastCheckpointID,\n\t\t\"condensation should use PendingCheckpointID, not generate a new one\")\n}\n\n// TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch verifies that\n// HandleTurnEnd does NOT delete the shadow branch when another active\n// session shares it.\nfunc TestTurnEnd_ConcurrentSession_PreservesShadowBranch(t *testing.T) {\n\tdir := setupGitRepo(t)\n\tt.Chdir(dir)\n\n\trepo, err := git.PlainOpen(dir)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\ts := &ManualCommitStrategy{}\n\tsessionID1 := \"test-turnend-concurrent-1\"\n\tsessionID2 := \"test-turnend-concurrent-2\"\n\n\t// Initialize first session and save a checkpoint\n\tsetupSessionWithCheckpoint(t, s, repo, dir, sessionID1)\n\n\t// Get worktree info from first session\n\tstate1, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tworktreePath := state1.WorktreePath\n\tbaseCommit := state1.BaseCommit\n\tworktreeID := state1.WorktreeID\n\n\t// Transition first session through PostCommit to ACTIVE_COMMITTED\n\tstate1.Phase = session.PhaseActive\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state1))\n\n\tcommitWithCheckpointTrailer(t, repo, dir, \"e5f6a1b2c3d4\")\n\n\terr = s.PostCommit()\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\tstate1, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID1)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\trequire.Equal(t, session.PhaseActiveCommitted, state1.Phase)\n\n\t// Create a second session with the SAME base commit and worktree (concurrent)\n\tnow := time.Now()\n\tstate2 := &SessionState{\n\t\tSessionID:           sessionID2,\n\t\tBaseCommit:          state1.BaseCommit, // Same base commit (post-migration)\n\t\tWorktreePath:        worktreePath,\n\t\tWorktreeID:          worktreeID,\n\t\tStartedAt:           now,\n\t\tPhase:               session.PhaseActive,\n\t\tLastInteractionTime: &now,\n\t\tStepCount:           1,\n\t}\n\trequire.NoError(t, s.saveSessionState(state2))\n\n\t// Record shadow branch name (shared by both sessions)\n\tshadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state1.BaseCommit, state1.WorktreeID)\n\n\t// First session ends its turn — should NOT delete shadow branch\n\tresult := session.Transition(state1.Phase, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})\n\tremaining := session.ApplyCommonActions(state1, result)\n\n\terr = s.HandleTurnEnd(state1, remaining)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\n\t// Verify shadow branch is preserved (second session is still active on it)\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\n\t// Note: the shadow branch at the OLD base commit (before migration) was deleted\n\t// by condensation. But the second session's base commit matches the NEW post-migration\n\t// base commit. Since condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on the first session,\n\t// we need to check the second session's branch.\n\tnewShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state2.BaseCommit, state2.WorktreeID)\n\tnewRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)\n\t// The shadow branch may or may not exist depending on whether session2 has made\n\t// any checkpoints. The key assertion is that condensation succeeded for session1:\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, state1.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be reset after condensation\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state1.Phase,\n\t\t\"first session should be IDLE after turn end\")\n\n\t// Verify second session is still active and unaffected\n\tstate2, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID2)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state2.Phase,\n\t\t\"second session should still be ACTIVE\")\n\t_ = baseCommit // used for documentation\n}\n\n// TestTurnEnd_ActiveCommitted_CondensesSession verifies that HandleTurnEnd\n// with ActionCondense (from ACTIVE_COMMITTED → IDLE) condenses the session\n// to entire/sessions and cleans up the shadow branch."
}
```

> AGENT

The two `repo.Reference` calls have results we're not checking. The shadow branch at the old BaseCommit was already condensed (and condenseAndUpdateState marks it for deletion). The key guard we're testing is that `hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch` prevents deletion. But after `condenseAndUpdateState`, `state1.BaseCommit` gets updated to HEAD, so the shadow branch name changes. The concurrent session check happens at the *original* BaseCommit. Let me simplify this test to focus on what matters:

> TOOL

```json
{

But the second session's base commit matches the NEW post-migration\n\t// base commit. Since condenseAndUpdateState updates BaseCommit on the first session,\n\t// we need to check the second session's branch.\n\tnewShadowBranch := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state2.BaseCommit, state2.WorktreeID)\n\tnewRefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(newShadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(newRefName, true)\n\t// The shadow branch may or may not exist depending on whether session2 has made\n\t// any checkpoints. The key assertion is that condensation succeeded for session1:\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, state1.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be reset after condensation\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state1.Phase,\n\t\t\"first session should be IDLE after turn end\")\n\n\t// Verify second session is still active and unaffected\n\tstate2, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID2)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state2.Phase,\n\t\t\"second session should still be ACTIVE\")\n\t_ = baseCommit // used for documentation",
  "new_string": "\t// Shadow branch at the pre-condensation BaseCommit should be preserved\n\t// because session2 is still active on it.\n\trefName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranch)\n\t_, err = repo.Reference(refName, true)\n\tassert.NoError(t, err,\n\t\t\"shadow branch should be preserved when another active session shares it\")\n\n\t// Condensation still succeeded for session1\n\tassert.Equal(t, 0, state1.StepCount,\n\t\t\"StepCount should be reset after condensation\")\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseIdle, state1.Phase,\n\t\t\"first session should be IDLE after turn end\")\n\n\t// Second session is still active and unaffected\n\tstate2, err = s.loadSessionState(sessionID2)\n\trequire.NoError(t, err)\n\tassert.Equal(t, session.PhaseActive, state2.Phase,\n\t\t\"second session should still be ACTIVE\")\n\t_ = baseCommit // used for documentation"
```

> AGENT

All three threads replied to. Here's the summary:

**Thread 1 (Low - amend handler):** Acknowledged as pre-existing, out of scope for this PR.

**Thread 2 (High - PendingCheckpointID mismatch):** Fixed in `b5e924cd`. PostCommit now stores `checkpointID` into `state.PendingCheckpointID` when `ActionMigrateShadowBranch` fires. Without this, HandleTurnEnd would generate a new ID that doesn't match the commit trailer, breaking bidirectional linking.

**Thread 3 (Medium - unsafe shadow branch deletion):** Fixed in `b5e924cd`. Added `hasOtherActiveSessionsOnBranch()` check before deletion, matching PostCommit's safety pattern.

Both real bugs had test coverage added to prevent regression.
